# IMPERIA COLD WAR — Kissinger statecraft and contested regional politics.
COLDWAR_USA_KS_diplomacy = {
	COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_beijing_accept = {
		icon = GFX_decision_generic_political_discourse
		visible = {
			tag = CHI
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_beijing_pending
		}
		available = {
			COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_beijing_accept_valid = yes
		}
		cost = 0
		complete_effect = {
			effect_tooltip = {
				custom_effect_tooltip = COLDWAR_USA_KS_if_accept_tt
				USA = { add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_beijing_usa
				add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_preview_foreign_influence_modifier = 0.05 tooltip = COLDWAR_USA_KS_mod_foreign_influence_modifier_tt }
				add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_preview_decryption_factor = 0.05 tooltip = COLDWAR_USA_KS_mod_decryption_factor_tt }
				add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_preview_weekly_gdp = -0.00002 tooltip = COLDWAR_USA_KS_weekly_gdp_5_tt }
				set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_preview_weekly_amount = gdp_total }
				multiply_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_preview_weekly_amount = -0.00002 }
				divide_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_preview_weekly_amount = 100 }
				add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_preview_weekly = COLDWAR_USA_KS_preview_weekly_amount tooltip = COLDWAR_USA_KS_weekly_tt } }
				CHI = { add_ideas = COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_beijing_partner
				add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_preview_encryption_factor = 0.05 tooltip = COLDWAR_USA_KS_mod_encryption_factor_tt }
				add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_preview_agency_upgrade_time = -0.05 tooltip = COLDWAR_USA_KS_mod_agency_upgrade_time_tt } }
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				COLDWAR_USA_KS_deal_beijing_accept = yes
			}
		}
		ai_will_do = { factor = 1 }
	}

		target_root_trigger = {
			tag = CHI
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_regional_pending
			USA = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_regional_pending NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_regional_signed } COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_relations = yes COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_regional_parent = yes }
		}
		target_trigger = {
			FROM = { exists = yes NOT = { tag = CHI } NOT = { tag = USA } OR = { has_attache_from = ROOT has_volunteers_amount_from = { tag = ROOT count > 0 } } }
		}
		cost = 0
		complete_effect = {
			hidden_effect = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						tag = CHI
						has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_regional_pending
						USA = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_regional_pending NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_regional_signed } COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_relations = yes COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_regional_parent = yes }
						FROM = { exists = yes NOT = { tag = CHI } NOT = { tag = USA } OR = { has_attache_from = ROOT has_volunteers_amount_from = { tag = ROOT count > 0 } } }
					}
					USA = { set_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_regional_target = FROM.id } add_to_variable = { COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_regional_revision = 1 } clear_variable = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_regional_approved_revision country_event = { id = COLDWAR_USA_TD.10 } }
					clear_variable = COLDWAR_USA_KS_td_regional_review_revision
				}
			}
		}
		ai_will_do = { factor = 0.1 }
	}
